Formal evidence is strong for precisely stated predicates/model classes, but correspondence to an engine and environmental assumptions require separate evidence.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational scheduler and owner of the promised service/result.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Availability cannot be conjured by assigning a role; escalating does not grant competence or permission.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate waiting reason, expected availability and the decision needed to change it.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>An unavailable-resource scenario produces a truthful wait/escalation/adaptation outcome rather than a false completion guarantee.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A structurally sound process waits forever or misses a necessary deadline.</p></li><li><p>Scheduling complexity and stale availability data; local utilisation optimisation can harm end-to-end service.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/62.md#r-62f349f280086ca9a99cfc3d133e342499564b194df43c3ca0087f4f63239de9">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 62f349f28008</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>GENERALISED: structural assurance is coupled to a separate feasibility argument rather than inflated into one.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Assess whether eligible resources and time assumptions can support the enabled work, and specify what happens when they cannot. <a id="EWM-EWM-021"></a>
## EWM:EWM-021

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-010.md#EWM-EWM-021">EWM:EWM-021</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EWM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EWM-021</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Data scope, bindings, ownership and access</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/0f.md#r-0fb34230d8e4ec86756f6b8b9b3285d2be5d22293442271c0100e3411c7692d7">Complete shared origin class · 0fb34230d8e4</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/97.md#r-9756fee1f18300f0fabb3fa911592a1b58d948c17eac2dd2130b1f56701d201f">Complete shared evidence limit · 9756fee1f183</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Task data flow, binding, ownership, freshness and concurrent-update semantics</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>The value read at a task boundary has a known scope, owner and binding time.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Define who reads/writes each relevant value, when binding occurs and whether values are copied, shared or referenced across scopes.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Multiple activities or actors share data or pass it through subprocess/service boundaries.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A single immutable task input/output document can replace general shared-variable infrastructure.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/0a.md#r-0a21010528cb0217d22fdabd824ba6ed6d6b99592734ec4358d9c347d950de3b">Complete shared prerequisites required input · 0a21010528cb</a></p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/97.md#r-97b79f17b04ccd1b03a3dcc872e479d3ff89c85ac81c6038da205973df89a02e">Complete shared assumptions · 97b79f17b04c</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Workflow scope is not legal or organisational authority; ## EWM:EWM-023

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-010.md#EWM-EWM-023">EWM:EWM-023</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EWM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EWM-023</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Data-centric and interacting-object lifecycle coordination</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/31.md#r-3188ca11c98c0e26447ab003c443151d0bdbae5c860301d3eab7a651dc706543">Complete shared origin class · 3188ca11c98c</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>CONTEXT_DEPENDENT</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/3b.md#r-3b69e5728b9ac855d38dbe2db2b9cbf8cd865d24a97e9307e86ce066295e706a">Complete shared evidence limit · 3b69e5728b9a</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Interacting business-object states and data-centric lifecycle coordination</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A change in one object enables/constrains related work without losing its own identity or lifecycle obligations.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Use available case data or interacting business-object states to determine permissible work when a single activity route would hide essential relationships.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Multiple related objects evolve asynchronously or work is naturally case/data-driven.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>An activity-centred process with explicit data bindings is preferable when it already represents the relationships clearly.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/1c.md#r-1cd61f1c2eebdcfafeb6767121f1f2ae91e2e3105f17a498c47989d0ec4fa664">Complete shared prerequisites required input · 1cd61f1c2eeb</a></p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/78.md#r-788619b8bd044ac76061c97df3e44ed41d1c14f853b1fa6ba8b50d1bad740d56">Complete shared assumptions · 788619b8bd04</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Object existence does not imply authority to modify its state.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate relevant object/state changes to affected task and case consumers.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Case worker and the coordinator of interacting business objects.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A change in one object enables/constrains related work without losing its own identity or lifecycle obligations.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></li><li><p>Cross-object interactions may enlarge the state space; wholesale case visibility can disclose excessive information.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/8c.md#r-8c1f564bd63f6932ece4ef64ae6e63383513f13631d84d9476e47fe81fa01dd4">Complete shared criticism · 8c1f564bd63f</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-007.md#EWM-EWM-S007">EWM:EWM-S007</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-008.md#EWM-EWM-S022">EWM:EWM-S022</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-009.md#EWM-EWM-S034">EWM:EWM-S034</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-010.md#EWM-EWM-S039">EWM:EWM-S039</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM:EWM-R014</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>An activity-centred process with explicit data bindings is preferable when it already represents the relationships clearly.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. The consequential failure is One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Use available case data or interacting business-object states to determine permissible work when a single activity route would hide essential relationships.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Multiple related objects evolve asynchronously or work is naturally case/data-driven.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>An activity-centred process with explicit data bindings is preferable when it already represents the relationships clearly.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/1c.md#r-1cd61f1c2eebdcfafeb6767121f1f2ae91e2e3105f17a498c47989d0ec4fa664">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · 1cd61f1c2eeb</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/8a.md#r-8a9198e7a013884d1e21644bc714f09426c54106fb6eab38d867a679a531a882">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 8a9198e7a013</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Data patterns and data-centred models identify information/identity mechanisms. Field prevalence and economic effect of a particular binding policy remain poorly established by the inspected corpus.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Case worker and the coordinator of interacting business objects.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Object existence does not imply authority to modify its state.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate relevant object/state changes to affected task and case consumers.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>A change in one object enables/constrains related work without losing its own identity or lifecycle obligations.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></li><li><p>Cross-object interactions may enlarge the state space; wholesale case visibility can disclose excessive information.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/8c.md#r-8c1f564bd63f6932ece4ef64ae6e63383513f13631d84d9476e47fe81fa01dd4">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 8c1f564bd63f</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>ALTERNATIVE: data-centric forms complement rather than universally replace activity routing.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Use available case data or interacting business-object states to determine permissible work when a single activity route would hide essential relationships. Retain for fitting object structures; reject the claim that all workflows should be recast as one artefact model.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>One artificial case sequence obscures many-to-many dependencies or forces irrelevant tasks.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>An activity-centred process with explicit data bindings is preferable when it already represents the relationships clearly.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>Multiple related objects evolve asynchronously or work is naturally case/data-driven.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Case worker and the coordinator of interacting business objects.</p></dd><dt>Cost of simplification</dt><dd><p>Removing the mechanism is acceptable only while the cheap path still establishes this claim: A change in one object enables/constrains related work without losing its own identity or lifecycle obligations.</p></dd><dt>Retirement or omission</dt><dd><p>Retire or omit the extra machinery when the trigger, consumer or relevant dependency disappears, after settling/transferring the outstanding work or evidence that still relies on it. An activity-centred process with explicit data bindings is preferable when it already represents the relationships clearly.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>CONTEXT_DEPENDENT</p></dd><dt>Property kind</dt><dd><p>OPERATING_MECHANISM</p></dd><dt>Evidence strength partitions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/3b.md#r-3b69e5728b9ac855d38dbe2db2b9cbf8cd865d24a97e9307e86ce066295e706a">Complete shared EVIDENCE STRENGTH PARTITIONS · 3b69e5728b9a</a></p></dd><dt>Primary source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S022</p></li><li><p>EWM-S039</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S034</p></li><li><p>EWM-S007</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Empirical or case source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S034</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Contrary evidence</dt><dd><dl><dt>Source criticism ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-C007</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S034</p></li><li><p>EWM-S007</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Analytical objection</dt><dd><p>Cross-object interactions may enlarge the state space; ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-G08</p></li><li><p>EWM-G13</p></li><li><p>EWM-G16</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S015</p></li><li><p>EWM-S019</p></li><li><p>EWM-S040</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Limit</dt><dd><p>Dates and exact inspected editions resolve in SOURCE_TABLE; this is not a claim of earliest invention.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>Role and user constraints determine eligible resource assignment.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Work is assigned to roles, substitutes or automated principals. The consequential failure is A task is assigned to someone who is named by a role but unable or not permitted to perform it.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>A task is assigned to someone who is named by a role but unable or not permitted to perform it.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Separate role eligibility, current authorisation, competence and availability; obtain each needed condition from an appropriate authority rather than from a worklist label.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Work is assigned to roles, substitutes or automated principals.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>A known qualified individual with an explicit agreement may need no organisational directory integration.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/d4.md#r-d47b8e5cad60840e94927f1e1a574ca2a9ddd074dbc371a97f9f1e0c2271150b">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · d47b8e5cad60</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/33.md#r-3346ab2464e01d4f747e769d3c55bb784971b9bae5fc0354ed5cde440ea9987a">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 3346ab2464e0</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: A task is assigned to someone who is named by a role but unable or not permitted to perform it.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Resource patterns distinguish lifecycle/assignment concepts; qualitative work studies and the assignment-satisfiability formulation answer different questions from timed human performance.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Allocator and intended performer.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Workflow assignment records do not create external legal, professional or organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Tell the performer what is offered, under whose delegation and with which constraints.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>An assigned performer can explain and evidence the relevant eligibility and authority boundary.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A task is assigned to someone who is named by a role but unable or not permitted to perform it.</p></li><li><p>Stale directories, inappropriate substitutions and over-broad role memberships.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/de.md#r-de23f717b78ab21b0b77155b3f553926f44451ce3849518ce582e9433cc2b26f">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · de23f717b78a</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: assignment semantics are coupled to authority/capability checks without attributing new powers to the engine.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Separate role eligibility, current authorisation, competence and availability; obtain each needed condition from an appropriate authority rather than from a worklist label.
